`src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Metadata/AioRuntimeProvider.cs`:
```csharp
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Integration;
using MediaBrowser.Controller.Entities;
using MediaBrowser.Controller.Entities.Movies;
using MediaBrowser.Controller.Entities.TV;
using MediaBrowser.Controller.Library;
using MediaBrowser.Controller.Providers;
using MediaBrowser.Model.Entities;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Metadata;

/// <summary>Sets the runtime of Currents movies and episodes from AIOMetadata. Remote providers cannot: Jellyfin's merge skips RunTimeTicks for videos.</summary>
public sealed class AioRuntimeProvider : ICustomMetadataProvider<Movie>, ICustomMetadataProvider<Episode>
{
    private readonly MetaCache _metas;
    private readonly CurrentsItemLocator _locator;

    public AioRuntimeProvider(MetaCache metas, CurrentsItemLocator locator)
    {
        _metas = metas;
        _locator = locator;
    }

    public string Name => "Currents runtime";

    public Task<ItemUpdateType> FetchAsync(Movie item, MetadataRefreshOptions options, CancellationToken cancellationToken) =>
        ApplyAsync(item, cancellationToken);

    public Task<ItemUpdateType> FetchAsync(Episode item, MetadataRefreshOptions options, CancellationToken cancellationToken) =>
        ApplyAsync(item, cancellationToken);

    private async Task<ItemUpdateType> ApplyAsync(Video item, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (item.RunTimeTicks is > 0
            || item.LockedFields.Contains(MetadataField.Runtime)
            || !_locator.TryGetTitle(item, out var title))
        {
            return ItemUpdateType.None;
        }

        var meta = await _metas.GetAsync(title.Type, title.SeriesId, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        if (MetaMapper.ParseRuntimeTicks(meta?.Runtime) is not { } ticks || ticks <= 0)
        {
            return ItemUpdateType.None;
        }

        item.RunTimeTicks = ticks;
        return ItemUpdateType.MetadataImport;
    }
}
```

Replace `GetAsync` in `src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Metadata/MetaCache.cs`. Add these fields:
```csharp
    private static readonly TimeSpan MissTtl = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1);
    private readonly TtlCache<string, bool> _misses;
    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, Lazy<Task<StremioMeta?>>> _inFlight = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
```
Initialise them in the constructor with `_misses = new TtlCache<string, bool>(time);`, add `using System.Collections.Concurrent;`, and replace `GetAsync`:
```csharp
    public async Task<StremioMeta?> GetAsync(string type, string id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
        var key = $"{type}/{id}";
        if (_cache.TryGet(key, out var hit))
        {
            return hit;
        }

        if (_misses.TryGet(key, out _)
            || !AioMetadataEndpoint.TryParse(_settings.Current.AioMetadataManifestUrl, out var endpoint, out _))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var lazy = _inFlight.GetOrAdd(key, _ => new Lazy<Task<StremioMeta?>>(() => FetchAsync(key, endpoint, type, id)));
        var fetch = lazy.Value;
        _ = fetch.ContinueWith(_ => _inFlight.TryRemove(new KeyValuePair<string, Lazy<Task<StremioMeta?>>>(key, lazy)), CancellationToken.None, TaskContinuationOptions.ExecuteSynchronously, TaskScheduler.Default);
        return await fetch.WaitAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
    }

    // Shared by concurrent callers, so it never observes one caller's cancellation; the HttpClient timeout bounds it.
    private async Task<StremioMeta?> FetchAsync(string key, AioMetadataEndpoint endpoint, string type, string id)
    {
        try
        {
            var meta = await _client.GetMetaAsync(endpoint, type, id, CancellationToken.None).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (meta is null)
            {
                _misses.Set(key, true, MissTtl);
                return null;
            }

            _cache.Set(key, meta, Ttl);
            return meta;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is AioMetadataException or HttpRequestException)
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not load AIOMetadata meta for {Type} {Id}", type, id);
            _misses.Set(key, true, MissTtl);
            return null;
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException ex)
        {
            // An HttpClient/per-attempt timeout (no caller token reaches this method): record a provider miss.
            _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Timed out loading AIOMetadata meta for {Type} {Id}", type, id);
            _misses.Set(key, true, MissTtl);
            return null;
        }
    }
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `UserStore`, `UserRecord`, `UserLayer`, `StreamProfileResolver` (Task 7); `StreamPreferences.Normalize` (Task 3); `JellyfinClaims` (Task 11); `IAioStreamsClient`, `AioStreamsCredentials` (M1).
- Produces:
  - `sealed record DirectoryUser(Guid Id, string Name)`, `interface IUserDirectory { IReadOnlyList<DirectoryUser> All(); }`, and `JellyfinUserDirectory(IUserManager)` (Integration), registered as `IUserDirectory`.

    - `Task<string?> ValidateAsync(IAioStreamsClient client, string manifestUrl, CancellationToken)` returns null when valid, else a masked message. It parses the URL, then makes a real search for `tt0111161` to prove the password works, so the config is "validated on save" (spec §6).
    - `string? HostOf(string? manifestUrl)`
  - Self-service routes, any signed-in user, scoped to the caller (`[Authorize]`, user from the token; API keys, which carry `Guid.Empty`, get 403):
    - `GET /Currents/user/settings` → `UserSettingsResponse`
    - `PUT /Currents/user/settings` with a `UserSettingsUpdate` body → `UserSettingsResponse`; 403 when self-service is off or locked, 400 for an invalid config.
  - Admin routes (`Policies.RequiresElevation`):
    - `GET /Currents/admin/users` → `IReadOnlyList<AdminUserRow>`
    - `PUT /Currents/admin/users/{userId}` with an `AdminUserUpdate` body → `AdminUserRow`; 404 for an unknown user.
  - Write-only credentials: responses carry only the AIOStreams **host** of a saved config, never the URL (spec §6 "Storage & secrets").
  - Update semantics:
    - `AioStreamsManifestUrl`: null keeps the saved value, `""` removes it, anything else is validated and saved.
    - `Preferences`: null keeps; `ClearPreferences` removes.
    - `AutoSelect`: null keeps; `ClearAutoSelect` removes.

```csharp
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text.Json;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Clients.AioStreams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Common;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Streams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests.TestSupport;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Users;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Web;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using Xunit;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests.Web;

public sealed class UserSettingsControllerTests : IDisposable
{
    private const string AliceUrl = "https://aio.alice.example.com/stremio/0b6c3c7e-1d2f-4a5b-9c8d-7e6f5a4b3c2d/alicepw/manifest.json";
    private static readonly Guid Alice = Guid.Parse("aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa");
    private readonly FakeSettings _settings = new();
    private readonly FakeAioStreamsClient _streams = new();
    private readonly UserStore _users;

    public UserSettingsControllerTests() => _users = new UserStore(_settings, NullLogger<UserStore>.Instance);

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (Directory.Exists(_settings.DataFolderPath))
        {
            Directory.Delete(_settings.DataFolderPath, recursive: true);
        }
    }

    internal static ControllerContext As(Guid? user)
    {
        var claims = user is { } id ? new[] { new Claim(JellyfinClaims.UserIdClaim, id.ToString("N")) } : [];
        return new ControllerContext { HttpContext = new DefaultHttpContext { User = new ClaimsPrincipal(new ClaimsIdentity(claims, "Custom")) } };
    }

    private UserSettingsController Create(Guid? user) =>
        new(_users, new StreamProfileResolver(_users, _settings), _streams, _settings) { ControllerContext = As(user) };

    private static T Ok<T>(ActionResult<T> result) => Assert.IsType<T>(Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(result.Result).Value);

    [Fact]
    public async Task User_saves_their_own_config_and_preferences_and_never_gets_the_url_back()
    {
        var update = new UserSettingsUpdate { AioStreamsManifestUrl = AliceUrl, Preferences = new StreamPreferences { ResolutionOrder = ["1080p"] }, AutoSelect = true };

        var saved = Ok(await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(update, CancellationToken.None));

        Assert.Equal("Self", saved.Source);
        Assert.Equal("aio.alice.example.com", saved.ConfigHost);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "1080p" }, saved.EffectivePreferences.ResolutionOrder);
        Assert.True(saved.EffectiveAutoSelect);
        Assert.Equal(AliceUrl, _users.Get(Alice).Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl);
        Assert.Equal(1, _streams.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("alicepw", JsonSerializer.Serialize(saved), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Null_keeps_and_empty_removes()
    {
        await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AioStreamsManifestUrl = AliceUrl, AutoSelect = true }, CancellationToken.None);

        await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { Preferences = new StreamPreferences { CachedOnly = true } }, CancellationToken.None);
        Assert.Equal(AliceUrl, _users.Get(Alice).Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl);
        Assert.True(_users.Get(Alice).Self.AutoSelect);

        var cleared = Ok(await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AioStreamsManifestUrl = string.Empty, ClearAutoSelect = true, ClearPreferences = true }, CancellationToken.None));
        Assert.Null(_users.Get(Alice).Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl);
        Assert.Null(_users.Get(Alice).Self.AutoSelect);
        Assert.Null(_users.Get(Alice).Self.Preferences);
        Assert.Null(cleared.ConfigHost);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Invalid_config_is_rejected_with_a_masked_reason_and_not_saved()
    {
        _streams.Exception = new AioStreamsException("Invalid password for https://aio.alice.example.com/stremio/0b6c3c7e-1d2f-4a5b-9c8d-7e6f5a4b3c2d/alicepw/manifest.json");

        var result = await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AioStreamsManifestUrl = AliceUrl }, CancellationToken.None);
        var unparsable = await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AioStreamsManifestUrl = "https://aio.example.com/stremio/u/alias" }, CancellationToken.None);

        var bad = Assert.IsType<BadRequestObjectResult>(result.Result);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("alicepw", ((StatusMessage)bad.Value!).Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.IsType<BadRequestObjectResult>(unparsable.Result);
        Assert.Null(_users.Get(Alice).Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Locked_users_and_disabled_self_service_cannot_edit()
    {
        _users.Update(Alice, r => r.LockSelfService = true);

        var locked = await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AutoSelect = true }, CancellationToken.None);
        Assert.Equal(403, Assert.IsType<ObjectResult>(locked.Result).StatusCode);
        Assert.False(Ok(Create(Alice).GetSettings()).CanEdit);

        _users.Update(Alice, r => r.LockSelfService = false);
        _settings.Current.AllowSelfService = false;
        var off = await Create(Alice).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate { AutoSelect = true }, CancellationToken.None);
        Assert.Equal(403, Assert.IsType<ObjectResult>(off.Result).StatusCode);
        Assert.Null(_users.Get(Alice).Self.AutoSelect);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Api_keys_and_anonymous_callers_have_no_settings()
    {
        Assert.Equal(403, Assert.IsType<StatusCodeResult>(Create(null).GetSettings().Result).StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(403, Assert.IsType<StatusCodeResult>((await Create(Guid.Empty).UpdateSettings(new UserSettingsUpdate(), CancellationToken.None)).Result).StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Disabled_user_sees_that_streams_are_off()
    {
        _users.Update(Alice, r => r.StreamsDisabled = true);

        var settings = Ok(Create(Alice).GetSettings());

        Assert.True(settings.StreamsDisabled);
        Assert.Equal("None", settings.Source);
    }
}
```

```csharp
namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Integration;

/// <summary>Jellyfin's users, sorted by name.</summary>
public sealed class JellyfinUserDirectory : IUserDirectory
{
```

`src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Web/ManifestValidator.cs`:
```csharp
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Clients.AioStreams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Common;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Web;

/// <summary>Checks an AIOStreams manifest URL before it is saved: it must parse and its password must work.</summary>
public static class ManifestValidator
{
    private const string ProbeTitle = "tt0111161";

    public static async Task<string?> ValidateAsync(IAioStreamsClient client, string manifestUrl, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!AioStreamsCredentials.TryParse(manifestUrl, out var credentials, out var error))
        {
            return error;
        }

        try
        {
            await client.SearchAsync(credentials, "movie", ProbeTitle, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
            return null;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is AioStreamsException or HttpRequestException
            || (ex is OperationCanceledException && !cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested))
        {
            return $"AIOStreams did not accept this config: {SecretMasker.Mask(ex.Message)}";
        }
    }

    public static string? HostOf(string? manifestUrl) =>
        AioStreamsCredentials.TryParse(manifestUrl, out var credentials, out _) ? credentials.BaseUri.Host : null;
}
```

```csharp
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Clients.AioStreams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Common;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Streams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Users;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Web;

/// <summary>Self-service settings for the signed-in user (spec §6). Every endpoint is scoped to the caller's own record.</summary>
[ApiController]
[Route("Currents/user")]
public sealed class UserSettingsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly UserStore _users;
    private readonly StreamProfileResolver _profiles;
    private readonly IAioStreamsClient _streams;
    private readonly ICurrentsSettings _settings;

    public UserSettingsController(UserStore users, StreamProfileResolver profiles, IAioStreamsClient streams, ICurrentsSettings settings)
    {
        _users = users;
        _profiles = profiles;
        _streams = streams;
        _settings = settings;
    }

    [HttpGet("settings")]
    [Authorize]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    public ActionResult<UserSettingsResponse> GetSettings()
    {
        var userId = JellyfinClaims.GetUserId(User);
        return userId == Guid.Empty ? StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden) : Ok(Describe(userId));
    }

    [HttpPut("settings")]
    [Authorize]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserSettingsResponse>> UpdateSettings([FromBody] UserSettingsUpdate update, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var userId = JellyfinClaims.GetUserId(User);
        if (userId == Guid.Empty)
        {
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden);
        }

        if (!CanEdit(_users.Get(userId)))
        {
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden, new StatusMessage("Your Currents settings are managed by your server admin."));
        }

        var url = update.AioStreamsManifestUrl?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) && await ManifestValidator.ValidateAsync(_streams, url, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false) is { } error)
        {
            return BadRequest(new StatusMessage(SecretMasker.Mask(error)));
        }

        update.Preferences?.Normalize();
        _users.Update(userId, record => Apply(record.Self, url, update.Preferences, update.ClearPreferences, update.AutoSelect, update.ClearAutoSelect));
        return Ok(Describe(userId));
    }

    internal static void Apply(UserLayer layer, string? url, StreamPreferences? preferences, bool clearPreferences, bool? autoSelect, bool clearAutoSelect)
    {
        if (url is not null)
        {
            layer.AioStreamsManifestUrl = url.Length == 0 ? null : url;
        }

        if (clearPreferences)
        {
            layer.Preferences = null;
        }
        else if (preferences is not null)
        {
            layer.Preferences = preferences;
        }

        if (clearAutoSelect)
        {
            layer.AutoSelect = null;
        }
        else if (autoSelect is not null)
        {
            layer.AutoSelect = autoSelect;
        }
    }

    private bool CanEdit(UserRecord record) => _settings.Current.AllowSelfService && !record.LockSelfService;

    private UserSettingsResponse Describe(Guid userId)
    {
        var record = _users.Get(userId);
        var profile = _profiles.For(userId);
        return new UserSettingsResponse(
            CanEdit(record),
            record.StreamsDisabled,
            profile.Source.ToString(),
            ManifestValidator.HostOf(record.Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl),
            record.Self.Preferences,
            record.Self.AutoSelect,
            profile.Preferences,
            profile.AutoSelect);
    }
}
```

`src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Web/AdminUsersController.cs`:
```csharp
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Clients.AioStreams;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Common;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Users;
using MediaBrowser.Common.Api;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Web;

/// <summary>Admin per-user controls (spec §6): override config, lock self-service, disable streams or search auto-add.</summary>
[ApiController]
[Route("Currents/admin/users")]
[Authorize(Policy = Policies.RequiresElevation)]
public sealed class AdminUsersController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IUserDirectory _directory;
    private readonly UserStore _users;
    private readonly StreamProfileResolver _profiles;
    private readonly IAioStreamsClient _streams;

    public AdminUsersController(IUserDirectory directory, UserStore users, StreamProfileResolver profiles, IAioStreamsClient streams)
    {
        _directory = directory;
        _users = users;
        _profiles = profiles;
        _streams = streams;
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<AdminUserRow>> List() =>
        Ok(_directory.All().OrderBy(u => u.Name, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).Select(Row).ToList());

    [HttpPut("{userId}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AdminUserRow>> Update([FromRoute] Guid userId, [FromBody] AdminUserUpdate update, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var user = _directory.All().FirstOrDefault(u => u.Id == userId);
        if (user is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var url = update.AioStreamsManifestUrl?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) && await ManifestValidator.ValidateAsync(_streams, url, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false) is { } error)
        {
            return BadRequest(new StatusMessage(SecretMasker.Mask(error)));
        }

        update.Preferences?.Normalize();
        _users.Update(userId, record =>
        {
            UserSettingsController.Apply(record.Admin, url, update.Preferences, update.ClearPreferences, update.AutoSelect, update.ClearAutoSelect);
            record.LockSelfService = update.LockSelfService;
            record.StreamsDisabled = update.StreamsDisabled;
            record.SearchAutoAddDisabled = update.SearchAutoAddDisabled;
        });
        return Ok(Row(user));
    }

    private AdminUserRow Row(DirectoryUser user)
    {
        var record = _users.Get(user.Id);
        return new AdminUserRow(
            user.Id.ToString("N"),
            user.Name,
            _profiles.For(user.Id).Source.ToString(),
            ManifestValidator.HostOf(record.Admin.AioStreamsManifestUrl),
            ManifestValidator.HostOf(record.Self.AioStreamsManifestUrl),
            record.Admin.Preferences,
            record.Admin.AutoSelect,
            record.LockSelfService,
            record.StreamsDisabled,
            record.SearchAutoAddDisabled);
    }
}
```

### Task 18: The Currents user page and the admin page's versions and users sections

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Web/userPage.html`
- Modify: `src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.csproj` (embed `Web/userPage.html`)
- Modify: `src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Web/UserSettingsController.cs` (serve the page)
- Modify: `src/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents/Configuration/configPage.html`
- Test: `tests/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests/Web/PagesTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Task 17 routes; `PluginConfiguration` M2 fields (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `GET /Currents/user` (anonymous), which returns the embedded HTML page. The page reads the signed-in session from `localStorage['jellyfin_credentials']` (the matching server's `AccessToken`). It calls the Task 17 endpoints with `Authorization: MediaBrowser Token="…"` and sends no `Device`/`Version` fields, which would rewrite the user's web device record (research Q8). With no session it sends the user to the Jellyfin login.
  - Admin page sections:
    - "Versions": `EnableVersions`, `AllowSelfService`, `DefaultAutoSelect`, `MaxVersions`, `StreamCacheMinutes`, default preferences, and the self-service URL to paste into Jellyfin's `menuLinks`.
    - "Users": per-user override URL, preferences summary, auto-select, lock, disable streams, disable search auto-add.

`tests/Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests/Web/PagesTests.cs`:
```csharp
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests.TestSupport;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Users;
using Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Web;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using Xunit;

namespace Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Tests.Web;

public sealed class PagesTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly FakeSettings _settings = new();

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (Directory.Exists(_settings.DataFolderPath))
        {
            Directory.Delete(_settings.DataFolderPath, recursive: true);
        }
    }

    private static string Resource(string name)
    {
        using var stream = typeof(UserSettingsController).Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(name)
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException($"{name} is not embedded");
        using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);
        return reader.ReadToEnd();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void User_page_is_served_as_html_without_auth()
    {
        var users = new UserStore(_settings, NullLogger<UserStore>.Instance);
        var controller = new UserSettingsController(users, new StreamProfileResolver(users, _settings), new FakeAioStreamsClient(), _settings);

        var result = Assert.IsType<FileStreamResult>(controller.Page());

        Assert.Equal("text/html; charset=utf-8", result.ContentType);
        using var reader = new StreamReader(result.FileStream);
        var html = reader.ReadToEnd();
        Assert.Contains("jellyfin_credentials", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("MediaBrowser Token=", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("Currents/user/settings", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Device=", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Admin_page_has_the_versions_and_users_sections()
    {
        var html = Resource("Jellyfin.Plugin.Currents.Configuration.configPage.html");

        foreach (var id in new[] { "EnableVersions", "AllowSelfService", "DefaultAutoSelect", "MaxVersions", "StreamCacheMinutes", "DefaultResolutionOrder", "UsersTable", "SelfServiceUrl" })
        {
            Assert.Contains($"id=\"{id}\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }

        Assert.Contains("Currents/admin/users", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }
}
```

```csharp
    /// <summary>Serves the self-service page. Anonymous: a browser navigation carries no Jellyfin auth header; the page's API calls do.</summary>
    /// <returns>The HTML page.</returns>
    [HttpGet]
    [AllowAnonymous]
    [Produces("text/html")]
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Currents settings</title>
  <style>
    :root { color-scheme: dark; }
    body { margin: 0; font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif; background: #101010; color: #e8e8e8; }
    main { max-width: 42rem; margin: 0 auto; padding: 1.5rem 1rem 4rem; }
    h1 { font-size: 1.6rem; margin: 0 0 .25rem; }
    h2 { font-size: 1.1rem; margin: 2rem 0 .5rem; }
    p.hint, .hint { color: #a8a8a8; font-size: .9rem; }
    label { display: block; margin: .9rem 0 .3rem; }
    input[type=text], input[type=url], input[type=number], select { width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; padding: .55rem .6rem; border-radius: .3rem; border: 1px solid #3a3a3a; background: #1c1c1c; color: inherit; font: inherit; }
    .check { display: flex; gap: .5rem; align-items: center; margin-top: .9rem; }
    .check label { margin: 0; }
    button { margin: 1.5rem .5rem 0 0; padding: .6rem 1.1rem; border: 0; border-radius: .3rem; background: #00a4dc; color: #fff; font: inherit; cursor: pointer; }
    button.secondary { background: #303030; }
    #status { margin-top: 1rem; min-height: 1.4em; }
    .error { color: #ff8a80; }
    a { color: #00a4dc; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main>
    <h1>Currents</h1>
    <p class="hint">Which streams you see on titles from Currents. <a id="back" href="#">Back to Jellyfin</a></p>
    <p id="summary"></p>

    <form id="form" hidden>
      <h2>Your AIOStreams</h2>
      <p class="hint" id="configState"></p>
      <label for="manifestUrl">AIOStreams manifest URL</label>
      <input type="url" id="manifestUrl" autocomplete="off" placeholder="https://…/stremio/&lt;uuid&gt;/&lt;password&gt;/manifest.json">
      <p class="hint">Leave empty to keep your saved config. It is checked with AIOStreams before saving and never shown again.</p>
      <button type="button" class="secondary" id="removeConfig">Use the server's config instead</button>

      <h2>Versions</h2>
      <div class="check"><input type="checkbox" id="autoSelect"><label for="autoSelect">Only show the best stream (hide the version list)</label></div>

      <h2>Preferences</h2>
      <p class="hint">Lists are comma-separated and in order of preference, for example <code>2160p, 1080p</code>. Leave a list empty for no preference.</p>
      <label for="resolutionOrder">Resolutions</label>
      <input type="text" id="resolutionOrder">
      <label for="hdr">HDR / Dolby Vision</label>
      <select id="hdr"><option value="Any">No preference</option><option value="Prefer">Prefer HDR</option><option value="Avoid">Avoid HDR (SDR screen)</option></select>
      <label for="audioLanguages">Audio languages</label>
      <input type="text" id="audioLanguages" placeholder="English, Japanese">
      <label for="subtitleLanguages">Subtitle languages</label>
      <input type="text" id="subtitleLanguages">
      <label for="maxSizeGb">Largest file (GB, 0 = no limit)</label>
      <input type="number" id="maxSizeGb" min="0" step="0.5">
      <label for="excludedResolutions">Never show these resolutions</label>
      <input type="text" id="excludedResolutions" placeholder="480p, 360p">
      <label for="excludedVisualTags">Never show these tags</label>
      <input type="text" id="excludedVisualTags" placeholder="3D">
      <div class="check"><input type="checkbox" id="cachedOnly"><label for="cachedOnly">Only cached debrid streams</label></div>

      <button type="submit">Save</button>
      <button type="button" class="secondary" id="resetPreferences">Reset preferences to the server default</button>
    </form>
    <div id="status" role="status"></div>
  </main>

  <script>
    (async () => {
      const base = location.pathname.replace(/\/Currents\/user\/?$/i, '');
      const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
      const status = (text, isError) => { $('status').textContent = text; $('status').className = isError ? 'error' : ''; };
      const list = (value) => value.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const join = (values) => (values || []).join(', ');
      $('back').href = `${base}/web/`;

      let creds = {};
      try { creds = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jellyfin_credentials') || '{}'); } catch { creds = {}; }
      let serverId = null;
      try { serverId = (await (await fetch(`${base}/System/Info/Public`)).json()).Id; } catch { serverId = null; }
      const servers = (creds.Servers || []).filter((s) => s.AccessToken);
      const server = servers.find((s) => s.Id === serverId)
        || servers.sort((a, b) => (b.DateLastAccessed || 0) - (a.DateLastAccessed || 0))[0];
      if (!server) { location.href = `${base}/web/#/login`; return; }

      // Token only: sending Device/Version would rewrite the user's web-client device record.
      const api = async (path, init = {}) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${base}/${path}`, {
          ...init,
          headers: { 'Authorization': `MediaBrowser Token="${server.AccessToken}"`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
        });
        const body = response.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('json') ? await response.json() : null;
        if (!response.ok) { throw new Error((body && (body.Message || body.message)) || `Request failed (${response.status})`); }
        return body;
      };

      const sourceText = { None: 'No streams are configured for you yet.', Default: "You're using the server's AIOStreams config.", Admin: 'Your admin assigned an AIOStreams config to you.', Self: "You're using your own AIOStreams config." };

      const fill = (s) => {
        $('summary').textContent = s.StreamsDisabled ? 'Your admin has turned streams off for your account.' : sourceText[s.Source] || '';
        $('form').hidden = !s.CanEdit;
        if (!s.CanEdit) { status('Your admin manages these settings.'); return; }
        $('configState').textContent = s.ConfigHost ? `Saved: your config on ${s.ConfigHost}.` : 'You have not saved your own config.';
        $('removeConfig').hidden = !s.ConfigHost;
        const p = s.EffectivePreferences;
        $('autoSelect').checked = !!s.EffectiveAutoSelect;
        $('resolutionOrder').value = join(p.ResolutionOrder);
        $('hdr').value = p.Hdr || 'Any';
        $('audioLanguages').value = join(p.AudioLanguages);
        $('subtitleLanguages').value = join(p.SubtitleLanguages);
        $('maxSizeGb').value = p.MaxSizeGb || 0;
        $('excludedResolutions').value = join(p.ExcludedResolutions);
        $('excludedVisualTags').value = join(p.ExcludedVisualTags);
        $('cachedOnly').checked = !!p.CachedOnly;
      };

      const save = async (update, done) => {
        status('Saving…');
        try { fill(await api('Currents/user/settings', { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify(update) })); $('manifestUrl').value = ''; status(done); }
        catch (e) { status(e.message, true); }
      };

      $('form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const url = $('manifestUrl').value.trim();
        save({
          AioStreamsManifestUrl: url || null,
          AutoSelect: $('autoSelect').checked,
          Preferences: {
            ResolutionOrder: list($('resolutionOrder').value),
            Hdr: $('hdr').value,
            AudioLanguages: list($('audioLanguages').value),
            SubtitleLanguages: list($('subtitleLanguages').value),
            MaxSizeGb: parseFloat($('maxSizeGb').value) || 0,
            ExcludedResolutions: list($('excludedResolutions').value),
            ExcludedVisualTags: list($('excludedVisualTags').value),
            CachedOnly: $('cachedOnly').checked
          }
        }, 'Saved. Reopen a title to see your versions.');
      });
      $('removeConfig').addEventListener('click', () => save({ AioStreamsManifestUrl: '' }, 'Removed your config.'));
      $('resetPreferences').addEventListener('click', () => save({ ClearPreferences: true, ClearAutoSelect: true }, 'Preferences reset.'));

      try { fill(await api('Currents/user/settings')); }
      catch (e) { status(e.message, true); }
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

In the page script, add these helpers after the existing `selectedCatalogs` function:
```js
        const splitList = (value) => value.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const joinList = (values) => (values || []).join(', ');

        function readDefaultPreferences() {
          return {
            ResolutionOrder: splitList($('#DefaultResolutionOrder').value),
            Hdr: $('#DefaultHdr').value,
            AudioLanguages: splitList($('#DefaultAudioLanguages').value),
            SubtitleLanguages: splitList($('#DefaultSubtitleLanguages').value),
            MaxSizeGb: parseFloat($('#DefaultMaxSizeGb').value) || 0,
            ExcludedResolutions: splitList($('#DefaultExcludedResolutions').value),
            ExcludedVisualTags: splitList($('#DefaultExcludedVisualTags').value),
            CachedOnly: $('#DefaultCachedOnly').checked
          };
        }

        function fillDefaultPreferences(p) {
          p = p || {};
          $('#DefaultResolutionOrder').value = joinList(p.ResolutionOrder);
          $('#DefaultHdr').value = p.Hdr || 'Any';
          $('#DefaultAudioLanguages').value = joinList(p.AudioLanguages);
          $('#DefaultSubtitleLanguages').value = joinList(p.SubtitleLanguages);
          $('#DefaultMaxSizeGb').value = p.MaxSizeGb || 0;
          $('#DefaultExcludedResolutions').value = joinList(p.ExcludedResolutions);
          $('#DefaultExcludedVisualTags').value = joinList(p.ExcludedVisualTags);
          $('#DefaultCachedOnly').checked = !!p.CachedOnly;
        }

        function renderUsers(rows) {
          const body = page.querySelector('#UsersTable tbody');
          body.innerHTML = '';
          for (const user of rows) {
            const row = document.createElement('tr');
            const using = user.Source === 'Self' ? `own (${user.OwnConfigHost})` : user.Source === 'Admin' ? `override (${user.OverrideHost})` : user.Source.toLowerCase();
            const url = document.createElement('input');
            url.type = 'url';
            url.placeholder = user.OverrideHost ? `saved on ${user.OverrideHost}; paste to replace, "-" to remove` : 'none';
            url.style.width = '100%';
            const box = (checked) => { const b = document.createElement('input'); b.type = 'checkbox'; b.checked = !!checked; return b; };
            const autoSelect = box(user.OverrideAutoSelect);
            const lock = box(user.LockSelfService);
            const off = box(user.StreamsDisabled);
            const searchOff = box(user.SearchAutoAddDisabled);
            const status = document.createElement('span');
            const save = document.createElement('button');
            save.type = 'button';
            save.textContent = 'Save';
            save.className = 'raised button-alt';
            save.addEventListener('click', () => {
              const value = url.value.trim();
              status.textContent = 'Saving…';
              ApiClient.ajax({
                type: 'PUT',
                url: ApiClient.getUrl('Currents/admin/users/' + user.Id),
                data: JSON.stringify({
                  AioStreamsManifestUrl: value === '-' ? '' : (value || null),
                  AutoSelect: autoSelect.checked,
                  LockSelfService: lock.checked,
                  StreamsDisabled: off.checked,
                  SearchAutoAddDisabled: searchOff.checked
                }),
                contentType: 'application/json',
                dataType: 'json'
              }).then(() => { status.textContent = 'Saved'; loadUsers(); })
                .catch((err) => errorText(err, 'Could not save.').then((msg) => { status.textContent = msg; }));
            });
            const cell = (child) => { const td = document.createElement('td'); td.append(child); return td; };
            row.append(cell(document.createTextNode(user.Name)), cell(document.createTextNode(using)), cell(url), cell(autoSelect), cell(lock), cell(off), cell(searchOff));
            const last = document.createElement('td');
            last.append(save, status);
            row.append(last);
            body.appendChild(row);
          }
        }

        function loadUsers() {
          ApiClient.getJSON(ApiClient.getUrl('Currents/admin/users')).then(renderUsers);
        }
```
In the `pageshow` handler, after `$('#FailoverAttempts').value = c.FailoverAttempts;` add:
```js
            $('#EnableVersions').checked = c.EnableVersions !== false;
            $('#AllowSelfService').checked = c.AllowSelfService !== false;
            $('#DefaultAutoSelect').checked = !!c.DefaultAutoSelect;
            $('#MaxVersions').value = c.MaxVersions || 20;
            $('#StreamCacheMinutes').value = c.StreamCacheMinutes || 60;
            fillDefaultPreferences(c.DefaultPreferences);
            setText('#SelfServiceUrl', 'Users change their own settings at ' + ApiClient.getUrl('Currents/user') + ' — add it to Jellyfin\'s web config "menuLinks" to show it in the menu.');
            loadUsers();
```
In the submit handler, after `config.FailoverAttempts = …;` add:
```js
          config.EnableVersions = $('#EnableVersions').checked;
          config.AllowSelfService = $('#AllowSelfService').checked;
          config.DefaultAutoSelect = $('#DefaultAutoSelect').checked;
          config.MaxVersions = parseInt($('#MaxVersions').value, 10) || 20;
          config.StreamCacheMinutes = parseInt($('#StreamCacheMinutes').value, 10) || 60;
          config.DefaultPreferences = readDefaultPreferences();
```
Also change the default AIOStreams field description from "Used for every user in this version." to "Used for every user without their own or an assigned config."

The `cell` helper inside `renderUsers` shadows any outer `cell` helper on purpose. If the existing script already defines a compatible `cell(node)`, reuse it and delete the inner one.

Expected: PASS; the build is clean.

### Task 19: Docs, spec amendments, version 0.2.0, and the dev-stack end-to-end check

**Files:**

- Modify: `docs/architecture.md`, `README.md`, `docs/client-matrix.md`
- Modify: `Directory.Build.props` (`0.2.0` / `0.2.0.0` / `0.2.0.0`), `build.yaml` (`changelog`)
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md` (the `StrmBaseUrl` rule: "in M1" → "in degraded mode")
- Create: `docs/spikes/2026-10-m2-e2e.md` (results of Step 4)

**Interfaces:** documentation and verification only. No new code unless Step 4 finds a defect, in which case write a failing test first, fix, and record it in the results file.

- [ ] **Step 1: Amend the spec** (each a short, dated "M2 amendment" note at the section it changes):
  - §4.3 Playback:
    - Version ids are derived from (item, **user**, stream), because Jellyfin looks versions up by id with no user (streaming, sessions, subtitles). A process-wide registry resolves them.
    - Client-facing `Path` values are redacted (`currents://version/{id}`); ffmpeg gets a loopback URL. Version URLs are served only to the server itself (loopback or its own addresses).
    - List views never search AIOStreams; only item detail and PlaybackInfo do.
    - PlaybackInfo maps stale or item-level `MediaSourceId`s to the user's version, probes that version when its parsed info is not enough, and saves the item runtime.
    - Pre-filled tracks use `Index = -1` and an estimated video bitrate.
  - §5.5 Probing:
    - The selected-version probe and the runtime fallback moved into M2. RemuxDB and probe persistence stay in M4.
    - Runtimes from AIOMetadata are set through a custom metadata provider, because Jellyfin's merge drops `RunTimeTicks` for videos.
  - §6 Self-service page: menu entry via jellyfin-web `config.json` `menuLinks` (no plugin API exists for a user menu entry).
  - §7: header-bound streams are still skipped; proxying moves to M4.

- [ ] **Step 2: Update docs**
  - `docs/architecture.md`:
    - New modules: `Users/`, the `Streams/` version pipeline, and `Integration/` (the decorator, filters, prober, locator, request context, internal URL).
    - A request walkthrough:
      1. item page
      2. version switch (`GET /Items/{versionId}` → base item)
      3. PlaybackInfo (filter → probe)
      4. HLS (decorator with token user)
      5. `/Currents/play/s/{token}` (loopback only)
      6. 302
  - `README.md`:
    - Per-user versions and the Version dropdown.
    - The self-service page at `/Currents/user`, and how to add it to the menu by editing the web client's `config.json`:
      ```json
      "menuLinks": [{ "name": "Currents", "icon": "tune", "url": "/Currents/user" }]
      ```
      In Docker the file is `/jellyfin/jellyfin-web/config.json`; it is overwritten on upgrade, so keep a copy.
    - That `StrmBaseUrl` now only matters for degraded mode.
  - `docs/client-matrix.md`: a row per client (Web, Android TV, Swiftfin, Findroid, Infuse) with columns "versions shown", "switch works", "plays", "resume". Fill in Web from Step 4; mark the rest "to test" (they need the maintainer's devices).

- [ ] **Step 4: End-to-end on the dev stack** (never the maintainer's personal Jellyfin; never print `dev/.env`)

Use the scripts' environment loading, as M1 did: `set -a; . dev/.env; set +a` inside the shell that runs a command. Never `cat` or echo the variables.

  1. Run `dev/deploy-plugin.sh`. Confirm Jellyfin restarts and the plugin shows as Currents 0.2.0.0 under `/Plugins` (API key from `.env`).
  2. Create users `alice` and `bob` (`POST /Users/New`, admin API key) with simple passwords.
     - Admin config: clear the default AIOStreams URL (`AioStreamsManifestUrl = ""`) and keep `EnableVersions = true`.
     - Alice: sign in (`POST /Users/AuthenticateByName`), then `PUT /Currents/user/settings` with `AIOSTREAMS_MANIFEST_URL` from `.env` in the JSON body, using Alice's token.
  3. **Isolation test (milestone exit criterion).** Pick a synced movie id and `GET /Items/{id}?userId=…` with each token:
     - Alice gets ≥ 2 `MediaSources`, named like `2160p … · cached · …`.
     - Bob gets exactly one, named "Streams are not configured…".
     - No `Path` in either response contains `/Currents/play/s/` or the AIOStreams/debrid hosts.
     - `GET /Items/{aliceVersionId}` with Bob's token gives Bob's view of the base item, not Alice's version. `POST /Items/{id}/PlaybackInfo` with Bob's token and `MediaSourceId=<aliceVersionId>` yields no playable Alice stream.
     - `GET /Currents/user/settings` never returns Alice's URL or password.
  4. **Routes and local-only:**
     - `GET /Items/{aliceVersionId}` with Alice's token returns the base item (status 200, `Id` = base id).
     - Hitting a version URL from the host (`curl` to `:8097/Currents/play/s/<token>`, where the caller is the Docker gateway, not loopback) returns 403. Take a token from an ffmpeg log line, or mint one with a temporary unit test; never print the token in the results file.
     - `GET /Currents/play/s/x.y` and `GET /Currents/play/movie/tt1` both route as expected (403 / 403-or-302).
  5. **Playback in the browser** (Playwright, jellyfin-web at `:8097`, signed in as Alice):
     - Open the movie; the Version dropdown lists the streams.
     - Pick the second version: no 404 in the console, and the selection stays.
     - Press Play: the player starts, and the server log shows PlaybackInfo, then (if needed) "Probed a version of … in N s", then ffmpeg reading `http://127.0.0.1:8096/Currents/play/s/***`. Record N.
     - Stop after about 60 s and reopen: "Resume" is offered at the right position.
     - Repeat for one episode.
  6. **Degraded mode:** set `EnableVersions = false` and set the default URL back. Open the movie: one source (the `.strm`); Play works as in M1. Restore the settings.
  7. **Logs:** `grep` the Jellyfin log for the AIOStreams UUID/password from `.env`, debrid hosts with keys, `/Currents/play/s/` followed by anything other than `***`, and `alicepw`-style secrets. Expected: no hits. Use a script that reads `.env` and reports only counts.
  8. Write `docs/spikes/2026-10-m2-e2e.md` with a pass/fail table of steps 1–7, the measured probe and first-play times, and any defects found and fixed. Record no secrets and no tokens.
  9. Commit: `git add docs && git commit -m "docs: M2 end-to-end results"`
